# Seedance 2.5 Prompt Library: 789 Prompts

English · [简体中文](LIBRARY.zh-CN.md) · [Back to the README](../README.md) · [See them with the clips on apimodels.app](https://apimodels.app/seedance-2-5-prompts)

This is the full prompt library of [Awesome Seedance 2.5 Prompts](../README.md): 789 Seedance 2.5 video prompts, 783 of them published by 199 creators on X (745) and Civitai (38) and quoted in full, word for word, with credit and a link to the original post, plus 6 reconstructions written by apimodels.app from finished clips and labelled as such.

Disclosure: this collection is curated by [apimodels.app](https://apimodels.app), the API platform I work on. apimodels.app offers [Seedance 2.5 through an API](https://apimodels.app/models/seedance-2.5); prices and limits are under [Where to run these prompts](../README.md#where-to-run-these-prompts). A browsable version with the clips playing inline is the [Seedance 2.5 prompt library on apimodels.app](https://apimodels.app/seedance-2-5-prompts).

## Contents

- [30s & Multi-Shot](#30s--multi-shot) (20)
- [Candid & Handheld](#candid--handheld) (120)
- [Dialogue & Lip Sync](#dialogue--lip-sync) (23)
- [Action & Fight](#action--fight) (171)
- [Cinematic & VFX](#cinematic--vfx) (269)
- [Animation & Anime](#animation--anime) (105)
- [Product & Ads](#product--ads) (44)
- [Reference & Consistency](#reference--consistency) (37)
- [How this collection was made](#how-this-collection-was-made)
- [Credits, copyright and licence](#credits-copyright-and-licence)

Each prompt has its own page under [library/](library) with the creator, the original post, where it was found, the language and length of the prompt, and a link to the clip. All 789 are also in [data/prompts.json](../data/prompts.json). The 6 reconstructions are marked "(reconstructed)" in the tables.

## How this collection was made

Posts on X were found through searches and through the [awesome-seedance](https://github.com/LearnPrompt/awesome-seedance) collection on GitHub (554 entries here were found via it). From that collection we took only the entries it marks explicitly as Seedance 2.5, and checked every one against the original post on X: the prompt is the text in the post itself or in the author's own replies under it, and entries whose text could not be found in the post were left out, because those are usually translations or rewrites. Civitai entries are the prompt in the post's public generation data, kept only when that data lists Seedance 2.5 and no LoRA or other add-on model.

6 entries (SD004, SD005, SD006, SD007, SD008 and SD009) are not creators' prompts: for clips where we had not found the creator's prompt, apimodels.app wrote one from frames of the finished clip, in English and Chinese. They describe the output, so they cannot recover negative constraints, exact dialogue or a reference-image workflow; they are writing references, labelled "reconstructed" on their pages, in the tables and in data/prompts.json. For SD006 the creator's own prompt for the same post has since been found, and it is in the library as SD058.

Left out: posts with no public prompt, posts that are not a usable prompt (ads, tutorials, news, comparisons of several models, chatter, fragments), promotions, prompts mixed with a lot of commentary, sexual content, anything involving minors, real people's likenesses, graphic gore, prompts under 25 characters, and duplicates. On X, a label in front of the prompt such as "Prompt:", trailing hashtags and t.co media links are not part of the stored text; the prompt itself is not edited. Titles and categories are ours, and the IDs follow the order of the library on apimodels.app, so SD001 to SD009 are the nine showcased in the README.

## Credits, copyright and licence

- **Every prompt published by a creator belongs to that creator**, and every clip stays with whoever made it. Each entry names the creator, links the original post, and links the clip where it is hosted; no clip is copied in by this library.
- **Quoted in full.** Every creator's prompt is quoted in full, word for word, as it appears in the original post. Check the original post before reusing a prompt commercially.
- **Our own material.** The 6 reconstructions are ours and, like the rest of the original showcase, under the repository's [MIT](../LICENSE) licence. The titles, the category tables and the data file of this library are licensed under [CC BY 4.0](../LICENSE-CC-BY-4.0). Please credit apimodels.app with a link.
- If you wrote one of these prompts and want it removed or the credit corrected, open an issue and we'll handle it promptly.
